Instead of modeling the full missing signal directly, we reformulate probabilistic imputation as a baseline-residual decomposition, where a pretrained model captures the dominant signal and a diffusion process models the residual uncertainty. To better exploit deterministic guidance, \model{} conditions the reverse denoising process on both the baseline-completed signal and its latent representation, while a reliability-aware conditioning mechanism adaptively controls the influence of baseline information during residual generation. This formulation simplifies the diffusion learning objective, enabling it to focus on structured correction terms rather than reconstructing the full signal. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDDMPI consistently improves both reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [211] [Musec: MomentUm SpEctral Clipping for Stable Muon-type Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11655)
*Zhuanghua Liu,Menglian Wang,Luo Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Musec优化器，用"谱裁剪"替代Muon的"谱展平"，从优化器层面、架构无关地解决Muon训练不稳定问题，并给出非凸非光滑随机优化下的首个收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大语言模型训练中常优于Adam/AdamW，但因其谱展平（把所有奇异值压到约1）易导致训练不稳定，表现为loss尖峰和权重无界增长。现有方法多依赖权重或注意力logit裁剪，需要针对特定架构修改，且无法直接解决所有模型组件的不稳定问题。

Method: 提出MomentUm SpEctral Clipping（Musec）：不再把动量矩阵的所有奇异值设为约1，而是仅裁剪超过阈值的奇异值，同时保留动量的谱结构，形成优化器级、架构无关的稳定机制。进一步提出Soft Musec，用耦合Newton–Schulz迭代近似的平滑谱饱和函数实现高效计算。

Result: 理论上，为Musec在非凸非光滑随机优化中建立收敛保证，据称是Muon类方法在该设定下的首个收敛保证。实验上，Soft Musec在广泛学习率与模型规模下相较现有Muon变体持续提升训练稳定性；在现有Muon变体发散的情形下仍保持稳定，同时在调参良好时性能与之一致。

Conclusion: Musec/Soft Musec通过对动量奇异值的阈值裁剪，为Muon类优化器提供通用且稳定的替代方案，兼顾稳定性与性能。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a highly effective optimizer for large language model training, often achieving superior convergence and performance compared with the widely adopted Adam and AdamW optimizers. Nevertheless, Muon is prone to training instability due to its spectral flattening, manifested by loss spikes and unbounded growth of model weights. Existing approaches primarily rely on weight or attention-logit clipping, which require architecture-specific modifications and do not directly address instability across all model components. We propose MomentUm SpEctral Clipping (Musec), which replaces Muon's spectral flattening with spectral clipping: rather than setting all singular values of the momentum matrix to approximately one, Musec clips singular values that exceed a threshold while preserving the underlying spectral structure of the momentum. Our strategy provides an optimizer-level, architecture-agnostic mechanism for stabilizing Muon training. We further develop Soft Musec, an efficient implementation that uses a smooth spectral saturation function approximated by coupled Newton-Schulz iterations. Theoretically, we establish convergence guarantees for Musec in nonconvex nonsmooth stochastic optimization. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence guarantee for Muon-type methods in the nonconvex nonsmooth setting. We provide empirical studies to show that Soft Musec consistently improves training stability over existing Muon variants across a wide range of learning rates and model sizes. Notably, Soft Musec remains stable in settings where existing Muon variants diverge, while matching their performance under well-tuned configurations.

</details>


### [212] [Learnware and AI Model Management System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11656)
*Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从"AI模型存储"向"AI模型管理"的范式转变，通过将管理单元从模型升级为"学件"（模型+规约）来构建学件坞系统（LDS），实现跨开发者、跨任务的模型识别、复用与组装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型池本质上只是"AI模型存储系统"，缺乏管理能力。由于模型开发者通常不愿共享训练数据，且现代AI模型的功能即使开发者本人也难以完全理解，因此系统如何在不访问训练数据甚至用户原始数据的前提下，识别并组装对特定任务有用的模型，成为根本性挑战。

Method: 提出"学件"概念：学件 = 模型 + 规约。规约通过机器学习过程生成，无需泄露开发者的训练数据，并具有理论上的数据保持性质。规约依据公开参考标准生成，因而在不同模型间可比较。基于此构建学件坞系统（LDS），并将规约作为AI模型协作协议，使独立开发的模型（含智能体）能够协同。

Result: 学件坞系统为构建强大的AI模型管理系统提供了路径。规约的可比较性使其可作为跨模型协作协议，支持独立开发模型的识别、复用、组装与协作。

Conclusion: 将管理单元从机器学习模型升级为学件，使原本看似不可达的AI模型管理目标成为可能，为不泄露数据前提下的模型管理与协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The transition from file storage to database management systems transformed stored data into managed resources. AI now faces an analogous transition from AI model storage to AI model management. Existing model pools essentially serve as \textit{AI model storage systems}. What is needed instead are \textit{AI model management systems} that enable models trained by different developers, for different tasks, with different data, and under different objectives to be identified, reused, and even assembled to address future user tasks. Because AI model developers are generally unwilling to share their training data, such systems should operate without accessing the training data of model developers and, ideally, without accessing raw data of future users. This requirement poses a fundamental challenge: the functionality of a modern AI model may not be fully understood even by the developer who trained it. How, then, can a system identify which models are useful for a given user task, let alone assemble models developed independently for different purposes? At first glance, this objective may appear unattainable. It becomes possible, however, by upgrading the basic unit of management from a machine learning model to a \textit{learnware}. \textit{Learnware = Model + Specification}. The specification, whose assignment transforms a trained model into a learnware, is generated with the help of a machine learning process without disclosing the training data of the developer and has a theoretically established data-preservation property. The \textit{Learnware Dock System (LDS)} provides a path toward powerful AI model management systems. Because specifications are generated according to a published reference and are comparable across models, they can also serve as an AI model \textit{collaboration protocol} through which independently developed models, including intelligent agents, can collaborate.

</details>


### [213] [Why Does Post-Training Quantization Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11716)
*Yuxiang Chen,Michael Beyer,Jun Zhu,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"训练后量化(PTQ)为何有效"，发现两个使预训练模型对量化鲁棒的机制：层新引入的误差与继承误差相互抵消（该性质在预训练中形成），以及LM-head几何结构优先保留高排名token的分数与概率。二者共同解释了量化误差经过多层传播后输出变化仍小的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化将LLM权重以低精度存储，每个量化权重都会给隐藏状态引入误差。按直觉，这些误差应随深度累积并破坏下一token预测；随机初始化模型确实会快速累积误差，但量化后的预训练模型隐藏状态误差累积很少，且下游任务性能基本保持，即使它们从未在量化噪声下训练过。这引出核心问题：为什么训练后量化有效？

Method: 作者对比全精度与量化前向传播，识别出两种刻画预训练模型量化鲁棒性的机制：（1）分析每层新引入误差与其从输入继承误差之间的关系；（2）分析LM-head的几何结构对token分数与概率的影响。并在多个模型和量化设置下验证这些发现。

Result: 第一，层新引入的误差倾向于与该层输入继承的误差相反，二者部分抵消，使全精度与量化传播之间的差异增长缓慢；这种抵消性残差交互在预训练过程中形成，定量分析表明它是减缓隐藏误差增长的主要因素。第二，LM-head几何结构优先保留高排名token的分数和概率，而这些通常是模型最自信的预测。

Conclusion: 这两个机制共同解释了为何经过众多层的量化误差仍只产生很小的输出变化，且该结论在不同模型和量化设置下均得到验证。

Abstract: Post-training quantization compresses large language models (LLMs) by storing their weights at reduced precision, and each quantized weight introduces an error into the hidden states. Naively, these errors should accumulate with depth and corrupt next-token prediction; randomly initialized models accumulate these discrepancies rapidly, whereas quantized pretrained models accumulate much less hidden-state error and largely maintain downstream task performance, even though they were never trained with quantization noise. This raises the question we address: why does post-training quantization work? Comparing full-precision and quantized forward passes, we identify two mechanisms that characterize pretrained quantization robustness. First, the error a layer newly introduces tends to oppose the error it inherits from the layer's input. The two cancel partially such that the discrepancy between full-precision and quantized passes grows slowly. This counteracting residual interaction develops during pretraining. Our quantitative analysis identifies it as a major factor slowing hidden-error growth. Second, LM-head geometry preferentially preserves the scores and probabilities of high-ranked tokens, which typically represent the model's most confident predictions. Together, these mechanisms explain why quantization error that passes through numerous layers can still produce only small output changes, and we verify the findings across models and quantization settings.

</details>


### [214] [Predicting Privacy Leakage from Weight Spectral Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11780)
*Richard J. Preen,Jim Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究神经网络谱指标（WeightWatcher 指标）能否作为成员推理攻击（MIA）脆弱性的廉价代理，避免训练昂贵影子模型。结果显示 stable rank 与 MIA 成功率强正相关，Log alpha-Norm 在低假阳性区间与 MIA 脆弱性稳定负相关，且相关性均强于泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的 MIA 需要训练计算昂贵的影子模型，难以进行大规模隐私评估；因此作者希望找到低成本、可扩展的隐私审计替代方案，即利用重尾自正则化框架下的谱指标来预测 MIA 脆弱性。

Method: 在图像和表格分类任务上评估多个 WeightWatcher 谱指标，并将它们与 MIA 隐私泄露的关系同传统泛化度量（如泛化差距）进行对比。

Result: 跨数据集上，stable rank 与整体 MIA 成功率呈强正相关；Log alpha-Norm 在低假阳性区间与 MIA 脆弱性呈一致负相关，且这两类关联均强于泛化差距所反映的关系。

Conclusion: 神经网络谱可能包含传统过拟合度量未充分捕捉的隐私泄露信息，因此谱分析是迈向可扩展隐私审计的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are widely used to audit the privacy disclosure risk of machine learning models, however current state-of-the-art attacks require training computationally expensive shadow models, making large-scale privacy evaluation impractical. In this work, we investigate whether inexpensive spectral metrics derived from the heavy-tailed self-regularisation framework can serve as proxies for MIA vulnerability. We evaluate several WeightWatcher spectral metrics on image and tabular classification tasks and compare their relationship with MIA privacy leakage against conventional measures of generalisation. Across datasets, stable rank exhibits a strong positive correlation with overall MIA success, while Log alpha-Norm shows a consistent negative correlation with MIA vulnerability at the low false-positive regime. These associations are observed to be stronger than those obtained using the generalisation gap. The results indicate that neural network spectra may contain information about privacy leakage that is not fully captured by conventional measures of overfitting, motivating spectral analysis as a promising direction for scalable privacy auditing.

</details>


### [215] [Dynamic language model representations for multi-objective reaction optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11790)
*Joshua W. Sin,David Ming Segura,Bojana Ranković,Siu Lun Chau,Marius D. R. Lutz,Andrea Anelli,Ryan P. Burwood,Kurt Püntener,Maximilian J. Notheis,Raphael Bigler,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出从文本动态学习反应表征，与高斯过程代理模型联合训练微调语言模型，在多目标贝叶斯优化框架下实现少实验次数的反应条件优化，并成功应用于镍/钯催化的交叉偶联及不对称氢化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标化学反应优化（收率、选择性、安全性等）依赖反应组分的表征方式，但现有方法存在不足：独热编码缺乏化学信息，分子描述符难以跨化学上不同的组分扩展。对于结构和功能多样的组分，如何构建共享表征并不明确，且每遇新反应体系都需重新设计，耗时费力。

Method: 绕过人工设计表征的步骤，直接从文本中动态学习反应表征。用微调的语言模型对反应条件的文本描述进行编码，并与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，在多目标贝叶斯优化循环中获得任务自适应的表征。

Result: 在镍和钯催化的交叉偶联中（顺序与并行实验模式），该方法比描述符库或独热编码在更少实验内达到优化收敛。前瞻性应用于涉及混合配体齿合度与多相添加剂的钯催化氰化反应，以及跨手性铱、钌催化剂家族的三目标不对称氢化：两轮高通量实验（192个反应，低于各设计空间的3%）即获得可直接放大至克级的条件，分离收率分别为94%和84%，后者对映体过量达99.6%。

Conclusion: 从文本动态学习的任务自适应反应表征可有效替代人工设计表征，在多类反应与多目标优化中显著提升实验效率，并能前瞻性地给出可放大的优质条件。

Abstract: Optimising chemical reactions across multiple objectives, such as yield, selectivity, and safety, is central to chemical synthesis, and model-driven approaches depend critically on how reaction components are represented. Established featurisations are either chemically uninformative, as with one-hot encodings, or, as with molecular descriptors, do not readily extend across chemically distinct components. For structurally and functionally diverse components, it is therefore unclear what a shared representation should contain. Constructing such a representation is itself a challenging research undertaking that must be revisited for each new reaction system. Here we bypass this step by learning the reaction representation dynamically from text. Textual descriptions of reaction conditions are encoded by a fine-tuned language model trained jointly with Gaussian process surrogates, yielding task-adaptive representations within a multi-objective Bayesian optimisation loop. Across nickel- and palladium-catalysed cross-couplings in both sequential and parallel experimentation regimes, this approach reaches optimisation convergence in fewer experiments than descriptor libraries or one-hot encoding. Applied prospectively to a palladium-catalysed cyanation spanning mixed ligand denticity and heterogeneous additives, and to a three-objective asymmetric hydrogenation across chiral iridium and ruthenium catalyst families, two rounds of high-throughput experimentation (192 reactions, under 3% of each design space) delivered conditions translating directly to gram scale in 94% and 84% isolated yield, the latter at 99.6% enantiomeric excess.

</details>


### [216] [Thinking with Looped Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11801)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Chanhyuk Lee,Floor Eijkelboom,Nicholas M. Boffi,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 looped flows，用局部去噪目标训练循环模型的递归计算，避免长程反向传播困难，并在推理时通过概率流积分实现更多计算换取更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型理论上可通过更多推理计算解决更难问题，但实际训练中反向传播只覆盖一次或少数几次更新，导致早期更新难以学会支持后续更新，训练困难。

Method: 将递归训练转化为局部去噪目标，通过逐步降低噪声水平和共享噪声引入时间关联，使模型学到可随时间传递有效计算的循环状态；推理时对由学习到的去噪器参数化的概率流速度进行积分，并结合循环状态，通过更细的时间网格增加计算量，支持从不同初始噪声采样得到多个有效预测。

Result: 在包括两个多解基准在内的六个推理基准上，looped flows 整体优于先前最先进的循环模型，在 ARC-AGI-1 上达到 58.8% 测试准确率，在 ARC-AGI-2 上达到 12.2%。

Conclusion: 用局部去噪目标训练递归并结合概率流积分推理，可在无需长程反向传播的情况下有效训练循环模型，并通过增加推理计算提升困难推理任务表现。

Abstract: Humans and machines often solve harder problems by spending more time on computation. In deep learning, looped models implement this idea during inference by recurrently updating a hidden state. In practice, however, their training backpropagates through only one or a few updates, making it hard to train early updates to support future ones. We propose looped flows, an approach that sidesteps this issue by training the recurrence with local denoising objectives. By imposing temporal association across denoising objectives through progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise, the model is incentivized to learn recurrent states that transfer useful computation over time, even when gradients cover only a few updates. We then formulate inference as integrating the velocity of a probability flow parameterized by the learned denoiser, coupled with recurrent states. This allows solving harder problems by spending more computation through a finer temporal grid and enables multiple valid predictions from different initial noise samples. Across six reasoning benchmarks including two multi-solution benchmarks, looped flows outperform prior state-of-the-art looped models overall, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

</details>


### [217] [Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11842)
*Luyi Jia,Boyan Zhang,Yilun Liu,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于纤维状最优传输的模型感知调度构造方法，通过将预测风险与系数路径动能作用结合，得到闭式最优时间分配，在DDPM与flow matching上一致超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与flow-matching的调度沿仿射概率路径混合信号与噪声，已有的最小化动能作用方法虽能解释强基线，但属于模型无关且忽略预测误差，需引入模型感知的调度构造。

Method: 在概率路径固定时间与状态上，兼容的信号/噪声分解构成仿射纤维；定义纤维状预测风险，即纤维内真实分解与预测器诱导分解间最优传输代价的平均。将该风险与系数路径动能作用结合，得到闭式最优时间分配，可推广至一般线性预测目标，风险剖面可从早期基线检查点估计。

Result: 在DDPM与flow matching上跨预测目标、训练配置、风险估计检查点、数据集与架构评估，模型感知调度一致超越强基线，CIFAR-10上flow matching在16次函数评估下FID相对降低38.6%。在动能参考坐标下，独立训练模型的纤维风险剖面归一化后高度对齐，训练中的调度形变也一致，显示经验普适性。

Conclusion: 预训练检查点诊断将归一化风险一致性扩展至更大的条件潜扩散与2-RF模型；冻结的解析分配模板无需进一步风险估计或模型特定拟合即可保留大部分模型感知改进。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. We define a fiberwise prediction risk by averaging optimal-transport costs between the true and predictor-induced decompositions within these fibers. On a fixed coefficient curve, combining this risk with coefficient-path kinetic action yields a closed-form optimal time allocation. This construction extends to general linear prediction targets, and the risk profile can be estimated from an early baseline checkpoint. We evaluate DDPMs and flow matching across prediction targets, training configurations, risk-estimation checkpoints, datasets, and architectures. Our model-aware schedules consistently outperform strong baselines, including a 38.6% relative FID reduction for flow matching on CIFAR-10 at 16 function evaluations. Each model-agnostic kinetic baseline determines its own kinetic reference coordinate. In these coordinates, fiberwise-risk profiles from independently trained models in different settings align closely after normalization to unit area. The resulting schedule deformations used in training also align, suggesting empirical universality across the evaluated models and settings. Pretrained-checkpoint diagnostics extend this normalized-risk agreement to larger conditional latent diffusion and 2-RF models. A frozen analytic allocation template retains most of the model-aware improvement without further risk estimation or model-specific fitting.

</details>


### [218] [AdamX: Cosine similarity meets gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11867)
*Francisco Caldas,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdamX，一种利用余弦相似度作为自适应机制来控制更新幅度的一阶优化器，并引入方差校正方案以促进训练早期更平滑的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶优化器在控制更新幅度和训练早期优化稳定性方面仍有改进空间，作者希望提出一种可扩展、与模型无关且易于集成的自适应优化方法。

Method: AdamX 将余弦相似度作为自适应机制来控制更新幅度，并引入方差校正方案以在训练早期实现更平滑的优化；该方法具有可扩展性、模型无关性，能直接接入现有训练流程。

Result: 在多种基准数据集和模型架构上，AdamX 达到了具有竞争力的收敛速度；性能以固定超参数预算下达到预设性能阈值所需的 epoch 数来衡量。

Conclusion: 实证结果表明 AdamX 是一种具有竞争力的优化器，代码与实验已开源于 https://github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX。

Abstract: We introduce AdamX, a first-order optimizer that incorporates cosine similarity as an adaptive mechanism for controlling update magnitudes. The proposed method is scalable, model-agnostic, and straightforward to integrate into existing training pipelines. We further introduce a variance rectification scheme that promotes smoother optimization during the early stages of training. Overall, we provide empirical evidence that AdamX achieves competitive convergence rates across a range of benchmark datasets and architectures. Performance is evaluated in terms of the number of epochs required to reach predefined performance thresholds under a fixed hyperparameter budget. Code and Experiments available at: https://github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX.

</details>


### [219] [The Last AI Built by Humans: Toward Genuine Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11873)
*Yi Duan,Ying Liu,Zirui Tang,Haodong Chen,Jun Zhou,Yumou Liu,Bangrui Xu,Yukai Wu,Sidi Chen,Yuhan Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyou Yu,Shaokun Han,Xuzhou Zhu,Le Zhou,Bolin Lu,Wei Zhou,Jiachen Liu,Nuozhou Fang,Jiaxin Tian,Ruoyu Chen,Yuxuan Li,Kai Zuo,Kaiyan Zhang,Jiantao Qiu,Conghui He,Guoliang Li,Bowen Zhou,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhoufutu Wen,Jihua Kang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出递归自我改进（RSI）概念，用HCI指标揭示现有LLM的问题，并给出从改进-执行自主到递归元改进的发展路线图，跨场景分析RSI需求与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM缺乏将经验与反馈转化为持久改进的能力，需通过RSI实现能力与改进过程本身的持续提升。

Method: 先用Headroom-Closed Index（HCI）诊断现有模型不足，再构建RSI概念框架与五阶段发展路线图，并跨场景（科学发现、具身智能、软件工程）对比分析，结合产业实践与初步实证证据。

Result: 通过HCI揭示现有LLM存在的问题；明确了RSI的层次化发展路径及各场景在需求与推进速度上的差异。

Conclusion: RSI研究需与实用系统对接，实现真正RSI仍面临多项关键挑战。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables AI systems to turn experience and feedback into persistent changes that improve both their capabilities and the process of future improvement. We first use the Headroom-Closed Index (HCI) to reveal the problems of existing LLMs, then introduce the RSI concept and its development roadmap: from improvement-execution autonomy, improvement-strategy autonomy, experience-acquisition autonomy, and environment-adaptation autonomy, to recursive meta-improvement. Next we examine RSI across scenarios (e.g., scientific discovery, embodied intelligence, software engineering), highlighting their distinct requirements and development speeds. Drawing on diverse industry practices and preliminary empirical evidence, we connect RSI research with practical systems and identify key challenges to achieving genuine RSI.

</details>


### [220] [CoRA-NAS: Coarse Ranking and Anchor-Residual Refinement for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11884)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRA-NAS两阶段框架，将静态代理排序先验与低成本学习曲线修正结合，实现跨搜索空间的鲁棒神经架构排序与选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零成本代理虽能廉价地对架构进行排序，但其可靠性在不同搜索空间间差异较大，缺乏跨空间的稳健性。

Method: CoRA-NAS分两阶段：CoRA-Rank通过等权重对数秩共识聚合容量与初始化结构代理，并使用无目标共识门控形成静态先验；CoRA-Refine在该先验下采样锚点，外推其早期验证曲线，并用ExtraTrees模型传播学习到的残差修正。修正成本约为全训练候选集的1%，且不使用全训练架构-准确率标签拟合排序器。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201、NAS-Bench-101、TransNAS-Bench-101和NATS-SSS上，CoRA-Refine的平均Spearman相关系数分别为0.946、0.715、0.786和0.894；最差空间相关系数0.715为对比方法中最高。在NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100上，选中架构准确率达73.32%，接近报告的真实最优73.37%。在纯尺寸空间中，修正弥补了静态先验相对参数量指标的不足，并在噪声范围内与最强容量代理持平。

Conclusion: 该框架兼顾跨空间排序鲁棒性与低成本架构选择能力。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies rank architectures cheaply, but their reliability varies across search spaces. We introduce CoRA-NAS (COarse Ranking + Anchor-residual), a two-stage framework combining a static ranking prior with low-cost learning-curve refinement. CoRA-Rank aggregates capacity and structure-at-initialization proxies through an equal-weight log-rank consensus and a target-free consensus gate. CoRA-Refine samples anchors across this prior, extrapolates their early validation curves, and propagates a learned residual correction with an ExtraTrees model. The refinement uses approximately 1% of the cost of fully training the candidate set. Fully trained architecture-accuracy labels are not used to fit the ranker. One configuration is used across spaces, with space-specific architecture encodings. Across NAS-Bench-201, NAS-Bench-101, TransNAS-Bench-101, and NATS-SSS, CoRA-Refine achieves mean Spearman correlations of 0.946, 0.715, 0.786, and 0.894, respectively. Its worst-space correlation of 0.715 is the highest among the compared methods. On NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100, its selected architecture reaches 73.32% accuracy, near the reported ground-truth best of 73.37%. On the pure size space, refinement recovers the static prior's shortfall relative to parameter count, while remaining tied with the strongest capacity proxies within noise. The resulting framework combines cross-space ranking robustness with low-cost architecture selection.

</details>


### [221] [CausalArena: Benchmarking Causal Discovery in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11897)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一且可演进的因果发现评测基准 CausalArena，涵盖多种结构因果模型家族与协议，揭示不同基准下方法排名显著变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现评估依赖结构因果模型（SCMs），但现有研究在图族、机制和评估协议上差异很大；因果发现基础模型（CDFMs）的出现使评估更复杂，因为性能可能来自预训练环境与测试 SCM 的重叠，而非真正的因果发现能力，导致固定合成基准上的结果难以解释。

Method: 提出 CausalArena 基准，在统一协议下构建多类 SCM：合成 SCM 提供结构与机制上的受控广度；语义操作 SCM 提供可人工审查、语义化的环境；公式化 SCM 检验显式科学机制下的发现能力；并加入公开真实世界数据集作为外部效度验证。对经典、神经网络和预训练方法进行实验比较。

Result: 实验显示方法在不同 SCM 家族和协议下出现显著排名变化，某一基准情景下的强表现不能可靠迁移到其他情景。这表明基准多样性和预训练—评估重叠是基础模型时代因果发现评估的核心挑战。

Conclusion: CausalArena 通过统一、可演进、多来源的基准设计，揭示当前因果发现评估的脆弱性与偏差来源，强调需要多样化基准并关注预训练与评测重叠问题。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from data and is fundamental to scientific reasoning and intervention-based decision making. Its evaluation relies heavily on structural causal models (SCMs), which specify a causal graph together with the mechanisms that generate data, yet existing studies differ substantially in graph families, mechanisms, and evaluation protocols. The emergence of causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) further complicates evaluation: performance may reflect not only causal discovery ability, but also overlap between pretraining environments and test SCMs, making results on fixed synthetic benchmarks difficult to interpret. We introduce CausalArena, a unified and evolvable benchmark for causal discovery under a common protocol. Synthetic SCMs supply controlled breadth over structures and mechanisms; semantic operational SCMs provide human-auditable, semantically grounded environments beyond standard synthetic generators; and formula-grounded SCMs test discovery under explicit scientific mechanisms. Public real-world datasets provide an additional external-validity check. Experiments across classical, neural, and pretrained methods reveal substantial ranking shifts across SCM families and protocols, showing that strong performance in one benchmark regime does not reliably transfer to others. These results highlight benchmark diversity and pretraining--evaluation overlap as central challenges for evaluating causal discovery in the foundation model era.

</details>


### [222] [TART: A Modular Tool for Technique-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11904)
*Akshaj Gupta,Hwi Joo Park,Andrea Guzman,Shamak Gowda,Samhita Konduri,Jiachen Lian,Robin Netzorg,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TART，一个模块化的四阶段音频到吉他谱生成流水线，在零样本设置下于多个基准上取得了优于先前基线的性能，并首次实现直接从吉他音频生成同时包含指法与演奏技巧标注的吉他谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动音乐转录（AMT）在吉他上受限于三大挑战：现有系统难以捕捉滑音、推弦、击打等表现性技巧；常将音符分配到错误的弦-品组合；且多在干净录音上训练，泛化到嘈杂真实音频的能力有限。

Method: 提出TART，一个模块化的四阶段音频到吉他谱流水线，包括：(1) 音频到MIDI的转录模型；(2) 表现性技巧分类器；(3) 用于弦-品分配的音频条件T5编码器-解码器；(4) 自动化吉他谱生成器。

Result: 在GuitarSet、EGDB及两个增强基准Noisy GuitarSet和Noisy EGDB上进行零样本评估。四个基准平均：音频到MIDI F50为81.35%（比最佳先前基线高6.67分），弦-品Tab F1为71.8%（高8.5分），端到端Tab F1为54.08%。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，TART是首个直接从吉他音频生成同时带有指法和表现性技巧标注的吉他谱的框架。

Abstract: Automatic Music Transcription (AMT) for guitar remains limited by three challenges: existing systems often fail to capture expressive techniques such as slides, bends, and percussive hits; they often assign notes to incorrect string-fret combinations; and they are typically trained on clean recordings, limiting their generalization to noisy real-world audio. To address these challenges, we propose TART, a modular four-stage audio-to-tablature pipeline consisting of (1) an audio-to-MIDI transcription model, (2) an expressive technique classifier, (3) an audio-conditioned T5 encoder-decoder for string-fret assignment, and (4) an automated tablature generator. We evaluate TART in a zero-shot setting on GuitarSet, EGDB, and two augmented benchmarks, Noisy GuitarSet and Noisy EGDB. Averaged across these four benchmarks, TART achieves 81.35% audio-to-MIDI F50 (+6.67 points over the best prior baseline), 71.8% string-fret Tab F1 (+8.5 points over the best prior baseline), and 54.08% end-to-end Tab F1. To our knowledge, TART is the first framework to generate guitar tablature with both fingering and expressive technique annotations directly from guitar audio.

</details>


### [223] [From Protocols to Evidence: Bounded Claims for AI in Service of the Common Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11910)
*Nitesh V. Chawla,Paulo Benanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"断裂测试"（rupture test）框架，将制度基线纳入AI系统评估，区分证据受限的部署与测量受限的治理，并给出RISE AI架构以支持有界限的负责任AI主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI不仅是治理难题，更会暴露制度在响应性、归属感、关怀与问责上的既有失灵。AI一经部署便成为对这些制度断裂的干预，可能修复、加剧、替代或掩盖问题。因此负责任AI必须同时评估系统与它所介入的制度断裂，并厘清现有协议（EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001）究竟确立了何种权力结构、测量应在何处停止。

Method: 借助《Magnifica Humanitas》以尊严、技术权力与共同善为核心的道德框架，构建"断裂测试"，把制度基线与系统评估相连接；提出两个核心概念：证据受限的部署（将主张限定于已实际评估的范围）与测量受限的治理（记录有利证据也无法推翻的约束）；并在这些界限内提出RISE AI架构，用于对责任、包容、安全与赋权作出有界限、基于证据的主张。

Result: 形成了一套连接制度基线与系统评估的断裂测试方法，以及RISE AI架构，界定了负责任AI主张的边界，明确了现有协议在权力关系与测量限度上的盲区。

Conclusion: 负责任AI不只依赖更好的工程，还需要制度修复以及持续的道德与政治判断。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence does more than create a governance problem. It can also reveal where institutions have already failed to provide responsiveness, belonging, care, and accountability. Once deployed, AI becomes an intervention in those conditions. It can repair, compound, substitute for, or conceal the failures it encounters. Responsible AI must therefore evaluate both the system and the institutional rupture into which it is introduced. The move from principles to protocols is already underway. The EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF, ISO/IEC 42001, and assurance practices translate commitments into roles, requirements, records, oversight, and assessment. The harder questions are what these protocols actually establish, whose power they leave untouched, and where measurement must stop. Pope Leo XIV's Magnifica Humanitas provides a broader moral frame centered on dignity, technological power, and the common good. Drawing on that frame, we develop a rupture test that links institutional baselines to system evaluation. We distinguish evidence-bounded deployment, which limits claims to what has actually been evaluated, from measurement-bounded governance, which records constraints that favorable evidence cannot override. Within those limits, RISE AI provides an architecture for making bounded, evidence-based claims about Responsibility, Inclusivity, Safety, and Empowerment. Responsible AI requires better engineering, institutional repair, and continued moral and political judgment.

</details>


### [224] [Data Scarcity and Model Sparsity: Mixtures-of-Experts Overfit More to Repeated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11917)
*Atindra Jha,Margaret Li,Jure Leskovec,Percy Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究数据重复训练对稀疏架构（MoE）的影响，发现MoE比稠密模型更快退化，退化程度随稀疏度增加，且取决于总参数量。通过强掩码正则化可缓解，但仍无法完全匹配全唯一数据训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人类书写文本枯竭，重复训练数据已成常规做法。已有工作研究了稠密Transformer的数据重复效应，但对近年主导的稀疏架构如MoE尚未充分探索，尽管其计算效率更高。因此需要系统研究稀疏性与数据重复之间的不良交互。

Method: 在单域和多域数据混合下，改变数据重复率；在不同MoE设置（专家数量、粒度）下进行实验；模型规模从80M到1B激活参数（8.5B总参数）；尝试现有正则化方法作为补救措施；分析MoE过拟合的内部机制（路由稳定性和专家特化）。

Result: MoE在数据重复下退化更快，且随稀疏度增加而加剧，由总参数量而非激活参数量决定。80M稠密模型可重复8倍以上而退化极小，MoE在4倍时即开始受损，32倍后性能低于稠密模型。部分正则化方法如dropout可缓解过拟合；强掩码正则化下MoE甚至可在64倍重复时超过稠密模型，但无方法能完全匹配全唯一数据训练效果。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了稀疏性与数据重复之间的不利交互，提供了过拟合核心机制的证据及其潜在补救措施，并建议未来方法可通过打破记忆模式来减少模型参数的过度特化。

Abstract: As the supply of human-written text is exhausted, it has become standard practice to repeat language model training data. Prior work has studied data repetition for densely activated Transformers, but the effects of data repetition remains largely unexplored for recently dominant sparse architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), despite their increased compute efficiency. We vary data repetition rates across single- and multi-domain data mixes, and across MoE settings, including expert count and granularity. We consistently find, for models ranging from 80M to 1B active (8.5B total) parameters, that MoEs degrade more rapidly under data repetition. This effect increases with sparsity, dictated by total rather than active parameters. While 80M dense models can repeat data over 8x with minimal degradation, MoEs instead begin to suffer at 4x, and deteriorate rapidly, ceding their performance benefits in all-unique data settings to underperform dense models after 32x. We experiment with existing regularization methods as a potential remedy. We find that some methods, such as dropout, can mitigate overfitting. In particular, with strong masking-based regularization, MoEs are able to outperform dense models even when data is repeated more than 64 times. However, no method fully matches the performance of all-unique training data. Finally, we analyze internal mechanisms correlated with MoE overfitting in high repetition regimes, and find that MoE routing universally stabilizes early in training, and that expert specialization correlates with overfitting to repeated data. In sum, our work addresses the adverse interactions between sparsity and data repetition: we present evidence for the core mechanisms of overfitting and its potential remediation, and suggest promising avenues for future methods to reduce over-specialization in model parameters by disrupting memorization patterns.

</details>


### [225] [General Quantification of Covariate and Concept Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11918)
*Hongbo Chen,Li Charlie Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分布偏移下泛化理论局限于理想化、不可估计问题，借助熵最优传输提出γ*-概念偏移与统一误差界，并给出可估计方法DataShifts。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习界理论仅适用于狭窄的理想化设定，且无法从样本中估计，导致理论与实际应用存在鸿沟；同时当源域与目标域支撑不匹配时，既有概念偏移定义会失效。

Method: 利用熵最优传输，提出γ*-概念偏移这一关键概念，推导出统一协变量偏移与γ*-概念偏移的通用误差界，适用于广泛损失函数、标签空间与随机标注；进一步设计具有集中性保证的估计量及DataShifts算法。

Result: 所提误差界统一了协变量与γ*-概念偏移，适用面广；估计量具备集中性保证；DataShifts算法能在多数应用中量化分布偏移并估计误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了分布偏移泛化理论与实际应用间的差距，为分析分布偏移下的学习误差提供了严谨且通用的工具。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a core challenge in modern machine learning, yet existing learning bound theory is limited to narrow, idealized settings and is non-estimable from samples. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theory and practical applications. We first show that existing definition of concept shift breaks when the source and target supports mismatch. Leveraging entropic optimal transport, we propose a key notion: $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, and derive a general error bound unifying covariate and $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, which applies to broad loss functions, label spaces, and stochastic labeling. We further develop estimators for these shifts with concentration guarantees, and the DataShifts algorithm, which can quantify distribution shifts and estimate the error bound in most applications - a rigorous and general tool for analyzing learning error under distribution shift.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [HuRo: Robotizing Human Videos for Scalable VLA Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10706)
*Jinho Jeong,Se June Joo,Jaehyun Kang,Dongyun Kim,Yena Kim,Hanjung Kim,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了机器人化人类视频能否为VLA策略预训练提供有效且可扩展的监督，构建了HuRo数据集并验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人数据昂贵，人类视频数据丰富多样且可扩展，但存在人类到机器人的具身差距（embodiment gap）。现有方法或只在任务匹配场景下机器人化视频，或分别处理观测与动作对齐，缺乏对机器人化人类视频能否有效预训练VLA策略的系统性检验。

Method: 开发了一套机器人化pipeline，将异构人类视频转化为与机器人对齐的观测和动作轨迹，同时推断跨标注层级缺失的中间信号。基于该pipeline构建了HuRo数据集，包含约63万机器人化episode和1.42亿处理帧，来源于五种人类视频源。

Result: 在四个真实世界操作任务上，增加机器人化预训练规模使整体完成率从51.5%提升到80.3%，空间与视觉偏移下的OOD完成率从34.9%提升到72.2%。消融实验表明，视觉机器人化提升了OOD鲁棒性，且带重定向动作的端到端预训练优于仅视觉迁移。

Conclusion: 机器人化人类视频能够为VLA策略预训练提供有效且可扩展的监督，特别是端到端预训练配合动作重定向能显著提升性能和OOD鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human video datasets have emerged as a compelling alternative to expensive real-robot data, offering rich diversity at scale. To bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap, existing approaches either robotize videos in task-matched settings or address observation and action alignment separately at scale. In this work, we systematically examine whether robotized human videos can provide effective and scalable supervision for pretraining vision-language-action (VLA) policies. To this end, we develop a robotization pipeline that converts heterogeneous human videos into robot-aligned observations and action trajectories while inferring missing intermediate signals across annotation levels. Using this pipeline, we construct the HuRo dataset, comprising about 630K robotized episodes and 142M processed frames from five human-video sources. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, increasing robotized pretraining scale improves overall completion from 51.5% to 80.3% and OOD completion under spatial and visual shifts from 34.9% to 72.2%. Ablations further show that visual robotization improves OOD robustness and that end-to-end pretraining with retargeted actions outperforms visual-only transfer. Code and data are released on our website: https://3587jjh.github.io/HuRo.

</details>


### [227] [When Information is Worth the Risk: Behavioral Valuation for Hazardous Robotic Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10726)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Aamodh Suresh,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“估值层”视角，在固定信念更新、传感器模型、物理风险模型和有限时域信息规划器的前提下，仅改变用于对可行路径排序的标量目标，从而研究危险环境下机器人探索中信息与风险的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险环境下的机器人探索需要在收集信息的同时承担风险：高信息量的路径可能暴露机器人于危险中，导致任务提前终止，从而阻断未来观测。因此，关键问题不仅是“哪里不确定性最大”，而是“降低不确定性是否值得承担相应风险”。

Method: 作者采用估值层框架，保持信念更新、传感器模型、物理风险模型与有限时域信息规划器不变，仅替换路径排序所用的标量目标函数。在该框架内，引入基于Prelec概率加权的风险增强行为信息目标，构建从保守到激进的、可解释的信息-风险估值族，并分析估值参数对路径选择的影响。

Result: 理论上，估值参数会在高信息/高风险路径与低信息/低风险路径之间形成切换边界，并在可行探索策略上诱导出变换后的Pareto前沿结构。大规模失败截断网格世界实验表明，仅估值本身即可重塑信息-风险前沿；香农信息规划仍是强原始信息基线，而风险感知目标可通过避免导致未来感知截断的失败来降低危险暴露与机器人损失；风险增强行为估值与标准风险感知基线相比具有Pareto竞争力，并提供可解释的保守与中间机制。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持这样一种框架：机器人不仅在行动能降低多少不确定性上进行推理，还应判断获得这一不确定性降低是否值得承担所需风险。

Abstract: Hazardous robotic exploration requires robots to map spatial risks, such as unsafe terrain, radiation, fire, mines, or structural damage, while operating where collecting information can itself cause failure. A highly informative path may expose the robot to hazards, terminate execution, and prevent future observations. Hazardous exploration therefore requires deciding not only where uncertainty is largest, but when reducing it is worth the risk. This paper introduces a valuation-layer view of this problem. We keep the belief update, sensor model, physical risk model, and finite-horizon informative planner fixed, and change only the scalar objective used to rank feasible paths. Within this framework, we introduce a risk-augmented Behavioral Information objective based on Prelec probability weighting, yielding an interpretable family of conservative-to-aggressive information-risk valuations. Theoretically, we show that valuation parameters create switching boundaries between high-information/high-risk and lower-information/lower-risk paths, and induce a transformed Pareto-frontier structure over feasible exploration policies. Large-scale failure-truncated grid-world experiments show that valuation alone reshapes the information-risk frontier. Shannon information planning remains a strong raw-information baseline, while risk-aware objectives can reduce hazard exposure and robot losses by avoiding failures that truncate future sensing. Risk-augmented Behavioral valuation is Pareto-competitive with standard risk-aware baselines and provides interpretable conservative and intermediate regimes. These results support a framework in which robots reason not only about how much uncertainty an action reduces, but whether that reduction is worth the risk required to obtain it.

</details>


### [228] [Lie-Algebraic Bell Recurrences for Arbitrary-Order Twist Jets and Parallel-Mechanism Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10748)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任意阶运动学构造方法，在统一的对偶螺旋框架下关联串联传播、并联机构闭环与刚体平台点场，并通过三个互补算例验证至四阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能在同一框架内统一处理串联、并联机构任意阶运动学，并精确映射到速度、加速度、急动、snap点场的方法。

Method: 以对偶螺旋框架为基础，将圆柱副作为本原物理块（转动副、移动副为特例）；用普通Bell多项式组织指数因子导数并保持非交换因子物理顺序；并联机构经重复Leibniz微分得到任意阶三角主被动闭环递推。

Result: 平台螺旋射流被精确映射到速度、加速度、急动、snap场的点独立仿射不变量；三个算例（3C链、RR+RRR球腕、Hunt型6-RUS）验证，独立微分与分支闭环一致至四阶，残差低于$10^{-12}$。

Conclusion: 该纯运动学公式适用于所选主被动划分正则的构型，可提供任意阶串联与并联机构运动学的统一表示。

Abstract: This paper develops an arbitrary-order kinematic construction that links serial propagation, parallel-mechanism closure, and rigid-platform point fields within one dual screw framework. A cylindrical joint is retained as one native physical block, with revolute and prismatic joints obtained as special cases. For each fixed joint axis, ordinary Bell polynomials organize the derivatives of the exponential factor; across a chain, the noncommuting factors remain in their physical order. Initial-frame prefix and terminal-resolved covariant formulas then produce equivalent representations of the serial twist jet. For a parallel mechanism, repeated Leibniz differentiation, with joint-level derivatives organized by Bell polynomials, yields an arbitrary-order triangular active-passive closure recurrence: the same passive Jacobian is solved at every derivative order at a regular configuration, while the right-hand side contains only prescribed active data and lower-order jets. The resulting platform twist jet is mapped exactly to the point-independent affine invariants of the velocity, acceleration, jerk, and snap fields. The validation is deliberately complementary: a generic 3C chain with noncoplanar axes and nonzero rotational and translational cylindrical coordinates tests ordered serial propagation, an RR+RRR spherical wrist tests active-passive closure, and a Hunt-type 6-RUS mechanism with six active revolute joints tests an independently reconstructed platform jet and its affine fields. Independent differentiation of the rigid motion, evaluation of the affine fields, and the differentiated branch closures all agree through fourth order with residuals below $10^{-12}$ in the corresponding SI units. The formulation is purely kinematic and applies at configurations where the selected active-passive partition is regular.

</details>


### [229] [Expressive Robotic Pianist: Mastering Complex Piano Repertoire with Graph-Mimic and Musical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10844)
*Yanhong Liang,Xianwei Liu,Chaojie Fu,Shaowen Cheng,Yanyan Yuan,Chengwei Zhuo,Xi Chen,Yongbin Jin,Wei Yang,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于强化学习的控制框架，结合图优化指法与物理声学模型，使灵巧机械手实现接近人类钢琴家表现力的演奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在演奏乐器时难以兼顾机械精度与艺术表现力，尤其是复现人类钢琴家流畅的指法转换与细腻的动态控制仍是难题。

Method: 采用基于强化学习的控制框架；核心是图优化策略，生成接近人类动作模式的预压与按键指法；并将控制系统与物理启发的声学模型耦合，通过调制按键速度复现乐谱中的动态变化。

Result: 定量评估显示该模型在手指形态相似度与动态速度准确性上显著优于基线；感知测试中，非专业听众更偏好本系统演奏且难以与人类演奏区分；跨多种音乐风格的实验表明在保持高音符级准确率的同时实现富有表现力的演奏。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人系统超越纯机械精度、将机器人音乐性提升至可与人类钢琴家媲美的表现力水平提供了稳健路径。

Abstract: Enabling robots to perform musical instruments with human-level expressivity represents a frontier in bridging the gap between mechanical precision and artistic interpretation. Despite advances in robotic dexterity, replicating the fluid finger transitions and nuanced dynamic control characteristic of human pianists remains a significant challenge. Through a reinforcement learning-based control framework, we demonstrate that a dexterous robotic hand can achieve high-fidelity performance across a diverse piano repertoire. Central to our approach is a graph-based optimization strategy that guides the robot to generate natural pre-press and key-press fingering strategies that closely resemble human movement patterns. To achieve expressive sound production, the control system is coupled with a physics-inspired acoustic model that modulates keypress velocity to accurately reproduce the dynamic variations specified in musical scores. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate that our expressive control model significantly outperforms baseline methods in both finger morphology similarity and dynamic velocity accuracy. In a perceptual test involving participants from diverse listener groups, performances generated by our system are significantly preferred over baseline robotic performances and are indistinguishable from human performances for non-professional audiences. Furthermore, extensive experiments across multiple musical styles confirm that our method maintains high note-level accuracy while achieving expressive performance. Our approach provides a robust pathway for robotic systems to move beyond mere mechanical accuracy, elevating robotic musicianship to a level of expressive performance comparable to human pianists.

</details>


### [230] [ReactHuman: A Physics-Grounded Benchmark for Human-Like Reactive Decision-Making in Embodied Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10895)
*Yizhan Li,Jianxin You,Mengyang Xiong,Yinhuan Chen,Zicheng Zhao,Dekun Wu,Dongqing Zhang,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ReactHuman，首个面向类人反应式决策的物理基础基准，让多模态大模型充当仿真人形机器人的决策核心以应对突发家庭危险，含17类事件、1000+可复现场景和五项指标评测。评测七个代表性 MLLM 显示反应式安全远未解决，且失败不随模型规模缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人若以多模态大模型为决策核心，必须具备对突发物理危险（如接住滑落的盘子、躲避掉落的刀）做出即时安全反应的能力。但现有评测仅通过视频问答被动考察直觉物理，或聚焦导航、重排等刻意长程任务，未衡量模型能否将物理理解转化为即时、安全关键的行动。因此需要一个能测量"物理理解→即时动作"的基准。

Method: 提出 ReactHuman 基准：被评 MLLM 作为仿真人形机器人的大脑，面对突发家庭危险。涵盖17类事件家族、1000+逐位可复现场景，真值来自240Hz刚体仿真的精确无标注数据，并包含外观与物理相矛盾（泡沫铁砧、钢苹果）的对抗性物体。设计五指标评测套件，从合理、安全、物理基础三轴评分，并实际执行每个已提交计划使决策产生可观测后果。用该框架评测七个代表性 MLLM。

Result: 反应式安全远未解决：模型约每三个危险就错处理一个；依据固定倾向而非观测场景行动；信任外观而非运动；即使所选动作正确，也会在米级尺度上错过拦截点；这些失败均不随模型规模增大而减小。

Conclusion: ReactHuman 提供了细粒度诊断与可扩展的训练信号，推动构建具有物理基础、安全意识的具身智能体。基准已公开于 Hugging Face。

Abstract: Reacting to sudden physical hazards (catching a slipping plate, dodging a falling knife) is both a meaningful test of embodied intelligence and a hard requirement for deploying multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as the decision coreof household robots. Existing evaluations, however, probe intuitive physics passively through question answering over videos, or target deliberate, long-horizon tasks such as navigation and rearrangement; none measure whether a model can turn physical understanding into immediate, safety-critical action. We introduce ReactHuman, the first physics-grounded benchmark for human-like reactive decision-making, in which the evaluated MLLM acts as the brain of a simulated humanoid facing sudden household hazards; it spans 17 event families and over 1,000 bit-for-bit reproducible scenes with exact, annotation-free ground truth derived from 240 Hz rigid-body simulation, including adversarial objects whose appearance contradicts their physics (a foam anvil, a steel apple). We further design a five-metric suite that scores each reaction along three axes: reasonable, safe, and physically grounded. We physically execute every committed plan so that decisions have observable consequences. With this harness we evaluate seven representative MLLMs. Results show that reactive safety is far from solved: models mishandle roughly one hazard in three, act from fixed dispositions rather than the observed scene, trust appearance over motion, and miss interception points at meter scale even when the chosen action is correct; none of these failures shrink with model scale. ReactHuman thus offers both a fine-grained diagnosis and a scalable training signal toward physically grounded, safety-aware embodied agents. The benchmark can be found here: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Alan123/reacthuman-benchmark-scaled

</details>


### [231] [Planning along Differentiable Charts of Constraint Manifolds with General-Purpose IK Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10905)
*Thomas Cohn,Seiji Shaw,Harel Biggie,Travis Manderson,Nicholas Roy,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用逆函数定理，从普通正运动学雅可比矩阵中恢复解析逆运动学（IK）参数化的梯度，从而使自动化生成的IKFast等IK函数可用于基于梯度的轨迹优化，并给出最小二乘域扩展与可达性约束的可优化描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在运动学等式约束下的轨迹规划，可行运动被限制在构型空间的零测度子流形上，需要特殊算法处理。用解析IK对可行构型集合进行参数化是很有前景的策略，而基于梯度的轨迹优化需要IK函数可微。但绝大多数IK函数由IKFast等自动化元求解器生成，难以修改为可微，这构成了主要障碍。

Method: 作者提出计算解析IK参数化梯度的新方法：利用逆函数定理，从普通正运动学雅可比矩阵中恢复所需的梯度，无需手动修改IK函数。此外，还提出最小二乘域扩展，以及可达性约束的适合优化的描述方式，以在可达工作空间之外保留梯度信号。

Result: 通过数值实验和下游任务验证了方法的有效性，包括硬件演示：RB-Y1机器人抓起一个盒子并将其放置在桌面上。

Conclusion: 该方法使得自动化生成的解析IK函数（如IKFast）无需修改即可获得可用梯度，从而支持受运动学约束的基于梯度的轨迹优化，并通过数值实验与真实硬件任务证明了实用性。

Abstract: Planning trajectories for robot manipulators under kinematic equality constraints restricts feasible motions to a measure-zero submanifold of the configuration space, requiring special algorithmic treatment. A promising strategy is parametrizing the set of feasible configurations using analytic inverse kinematics (IK). Bespoke analytic IK functions can be written to be differentiable, a necessary property for gradient-based trajectory optimization. But the vast majority of IK functions are computed by automated meta-solvers like IKFast, and are difficult to modify for differentiability. We present a new approach for computing gradients of analytic IK parameterizations: we leverage the inverse function theorem to recover the desired gradients from the ordinary forward kinematic Jacobian. Furthermore, we present a least-squares domain extension and an optimization-amenable description of the reachability constraint, which preserves gradient signal outside the reachable workspace. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach through numerical experiments and downstream tasks, including a hardware demonstration of an RB-Y1 picking up a box and placing it on a table. Project website: https://cohnt.github.io/inverse-function-theorem-parameterization/

</details>


### [232] [IMLE-VLA: Fast Single-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10915)
*Kian Hosseinkhani,Qinhe Peng,George Shramko,Mehran Aghabozorgi,Jianing Qian,Tristan Engst,Alireza Moazeni,Dinesh Jayaraman,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMLE-VLA用单步条件生成器（cIMLE）替代扩散/流匹配的多步迭代动作头，在保持多模态覆盖与泛化能力的同时大幅提升推理速度并改善机器人运动平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略的连续动作头依赖扩散或流匹配的多步迭代采样（如π0.5需10步Euler），造成推理瓶颈，导致机器人出现"走走停停"的运动、任务完成变慢。

Method: 提出IMLE-VLA，用经条件隐式最大似然估计（cIMLE）训练的单步条件生成器替换迭代式动作头。cIMLE目标促进多模态动作覆盖，避免朴素回归头的模式坍缩，同时完全消除多步采样。将其应用于π0.5。

Result: 推理频率提升3.67倍（55 Hz vs. 15 Hz），动作吞吐最高提升11倍；在LIBERO 40任务基准上平均成功率98.0%为最高，且推理频率领先；在LIBERO-plus测试时扰动下保持π0.5的鲁棒性；真机（Franka Emika Panda）四任务上运动更平滑（抖动降低2.2–3.0倍）、任务完成更快、每回合推理时间减少3.9–6.6倍。

Conclusion: 单步cIMLE动作头可在不牺牲多模态性与泛化能力的前提下消除迭代采样瓶颈，显著提升VLA策略的推理效率、动作平滑度与任务完成速度。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language backbones to achieve strong cross-task generalization. A leading design couples this backbone with a dedicated continuous action head trained via diffusion or flow matching. However, such heads rely on iterative multi-step sampling, for example 10 Euler steps in $π_{0.5}$. This creates an inference bottleneck that produces stop-and-go movement in the robot and slower task completion. We introduce IMLE-VLA, which replaces the iterative action head with a single-step conditional generator trained via conditional Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (cIMLE). The cIMLE objective promotes multimodal action coverage, avoiding the mode collapse of naive regression heads while eliminating multi-step sampling entirely. When IMLE-VLA is applied to $π_{0.5}$, it increases inference frequency 3.67x (55 Hz vs. 15 Hz), enabling up to 11x higher action throughput. On the 40-task LIBERO benchmark, IMLE-VLA achieves the highest average success rate (98.0%) among all baselines while leading in inference frequency. Under the test-time perturbations of LIBERO-plus, IMLE-VLA retains $π_{0.5}$'s robustness while other baselines degrade sharply, confirming that the cIMLE head preserves generalization. Real-world experiments on a Franka Emika Panda across four tasks demonstrate smoother motion (2.2x to 3.0x lower jerk) and faster task completion, with IMLE-VLA outperforming $π_{0.5}$ on every task and reducing average VLA inference time per episode by 3.9x to 6.6x. Videos and code are available at https://kianhk6.github.io/IMLE-VLA/

</details>


### [233] [ObstaDiff: Generalizable Diffusion Policy Learning via Obstacle-aware Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10918)
*Jiawen Wang,Kevin Yao,Khalid Jawed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ObstaDiff，一种分解式扩散策略框架，配备轻量级障碍物感知视觉编码器，用于杂乱真实场景下的机器人操作。在真实温室环境中进行每方法61次试验（共366次执行），ObstaDiff 达到75.41%平均任务成功率和8.20%平均障碍物碰撞率，优于代表性模仿学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法大多假设背景干净，且缺乏显式的障碍物感知运动生成机制，难以泛化到具有非结构化障碍物的杂乱真实场景（如农业温室）。

Method: 提出 ObstaDiff 分解式扩散策略框架，包含轻量级障碍物感知视觉编码器；提取"目标-障碍物-背景"的结构化表示，使下游对齐策略在生成朝向目标中心瓶颈位姿的末端执行器轨迹时能够对周围障碍物进行推理。

Result: 在真实机器人温室场景中每方法进行61次试验（共366次执行），ObstaDiff 取得75.41%的平均任务成功率和8.20%的平均障碍物碰撞率，优于代表性模仿学习基线。

Conclusion: ObstaDiff 有效提升了在杂乱农业场景中的泛化能力，验证了分解式扩散策略与显式障碍物感知表示在真实杂乱环境下的有效性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has achieved impressive results in robotic manipulation, yet most existing approaches assume clean backgrounds and lack explicit mechanisms for obstacle-aware motion generation. Extending such policies to cluttered, real-world scenes with unstructured obstacles remains a key generalization challenge. We present ObstaDiff, a decomposed diffusion-policy framework with a lightweight obstacle-aware visual encoder. ObstaDiff extracts a structured target-obstacle-background representation, enabling the downstream alignment policy to generate end-effector trajectories toward a target-centered bottleneck pose while reasoning about surrounding obstacles. We evaluate ObstaDiff on 61 real-robot greenhouse trials per method (366 executions in total). ObstaDiff achieves 75.41% average task success and 8.20% average obstacle collision rate, outperforming representative imitation-learning baselines and improving generalization in cluttered agricultural scenes.

</details>


### [234] [Testing Between the Test Cases: Proving End-to-End Steering in Conditions You Never Drove](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10951)
*Menuka Ghalan,Charles Rodgers,Zachary D. Asher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用形式化验证（bound propagation）替代/补充仿真测试，对CARLA中训练的端到端转向网络进行验证，证明其能在不重新驾驶的情况下发现仿真测试遗漏的失效条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的自动驾驶测试存在根本性困难：一个通过所有测试条件的模型仍可能在真实世界中失效，因为仿真测试无法穷尽所有扰动组合。形式化验证可以直接弥补这一缺口。

Method: 在CARLA的仿真高速公路和城市主干道上，分别训练两个小型端到端转向网络：一个仅用晴天条件训练，另一个用晴天、雾天、夜间和低日照条件训练。对四个模型均设定2.19英尺的车道偏离预算。在不重新驾驶的情况下，使用bound propagation（一种读取训练权重的形式化方法）计算在两张捕获图像之间的每一种扰动强度下转向能漂移多远。

Result: 单次计算即可覆盖远超实车测试活动的范围：在主干道上覆盖133个位姿，若每个位姿测试10种强度则需10^133种组合，而该方法在单块GPU上几分钟内完成。形式化验证不仅发现了仅用晴天训练的策略的失效条件（无需仿真测试即可发现），还为测试用例之间潜在的失效提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 形式化验证是仿真测试的可行补充，可作为自动驾驶验证与确认（V&V）流程的一部分加以采用。

Abstract: AI-based automated vehicle testing is challenging because a model that passes every test condition can still fail in the real world. Formal verification offers a way to directly address this gap. On a simulated highway and an arterial road we trained two small end-to-end steering networks each in CARLA, one on clear conditions alone and one on clear, fog, night and low sun. All four models were driven against a 2.19 ft lane-departure budget. Without driving again, we used bound propagation, a formal method that reads the trained weights, to compute how far steering can drift at every disturbance strength between two captured images. One calculation covers more than a campaign could drive: on the arterial it spans 133 poses, where ten intensities each would be 10^133 combinations, in minutes on one GPU. Not only did formal verification find conditions that broke the clear-trained policy without simulation testing, it provided some preliminary evidence for potential failures between the test cases. Our overall conclusion is that formal verification is a viable complement to simulation, and could be adopted as a part of verification and validation for automated driving.

</details>


### [235] [LTLDiff: Finite Linear Temporal Logic-Guided Data Generation and Diffusion Policies for Multi-agent Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11043)
*Chuhan Meng,Haiyan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LTLDiff结合有限线性时序逻辑（LTLf）与扩散策略，用于多智能体机器人操作的协调任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体操作任务需要满足任务级的时间、逻辑和安全约束。现有扩散策略存在不同步、动作顺序错误以及协调失败的问题。

Method: 利用大语言模型从自然语言指令学习LTLf公式，并采用抽象语法树表示实现固定维度向量嵌入；该逻辑嵌入作为条件用于逻辑引导的数据收集和扩散策略训练。

Result: 在多智能体操作任务实验中，LTLDiff相比基线取得了更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 结果验证了LTLDiff在多智能体协调操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent robotic manipulation tasks require coordination among agents to satisfy task-level temporal, logical, and safety constraints. Recently, diffusion policies have been used to perform the task. However, they still suffer from desynchronization, incorrect action ordering, and coordination failures in tasks that require simultaneous or sequential multi-agent interaction. Therefore, LTLDiff is proposed as a framework that combines Finite Linear Temporal Logic (LTLf) specification learning for both the generation of demonstrations and learning via diffusion policies. Each task has a specific LTLf formula that is learned from a set of natural language instructions using a large-scale language model. To enable a fixed-dimensional vector embedding of the learned specification from the language model, LTLf uses an abstract syntax tree representation scheme. This embedding of logic serves as a condition for (i) logic-guided data collection and (ii) diffusion-based policy training, encouraging trajectories that are consistent with the desired ordering and coordination requirements. Experiments on multi-agent LTLDiff manipulation tasks demonstrate improved task success rates compared to the baseline. Together, these contributions demonstrate the effectiveness of LTLDiff for coordinated multi-agent manipulation.

</details>


### [236] [Gait-Dependent Effects on Quadruped Locomotion for Load-Carrying using Passive Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11059)
*Giovanni B. Dessy,Claudio Semini,Victor Barasuol*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析被动机械臂的刚度-阻尼选择如何影响四足机器人负载行进，通过仿真比较欠阻尼与阻尼配置，并绘制步态-负载-刚度-阻尼映射图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动机械接口为四足负载携带提供了轻量替代方案，但其阻抗会将负载动力学与运动模式耦合，因此需要研究刚度-阻尼选择的影响。

Method: 在平地运动仿真中，比较阻尼与欠阻尼的被动臂阻抗配置，覆盖不同步态与负载条件；对爬行步态评估ZMP裕度，trot仅作为动态激励案例。

Result: 爬行步态下，欠阻尼阻抗会增加被动关节振荡，并可能降低相对支撑多边形的ZMP裕度；trot不用于直接ZMP裕度稳定性比较。

Conclusion: 研究以步态-负载-刚度-阻尼映射图总结结果，表明被动臂阻抗选择对负载行进稳定性有显著影响，爬行步态适合进行ZMP裕度评估。

Abstract: Passive mechanical interfaces offer a lightweight alternative to actuated manipulators for quadruped payload carrying, but their impedance directly couples the payload dynamics with the locomotion pattern. This paper analyzes how passive-arm stiffness-damping selection affects payload-carrying locomotion under different gait and payload conditions. We compare damped and underdamped passive-arm impedance configurations in simulation during flat-ground locomotion. For crawl gaits, where the support polygon remains well defined, the results show that underdamped impedance increases passive-joint oscillations and can reduce the ZMP margin with respect to the support polygon. Trot is retained as a dynamic excitation case for the passive arm, but it is not used for direct ZMP-margin stability comparison. The results are summarized in gait-payload-stiffness-damping maps, where ZMP-margin reduction is evaluated for crawl gaits and trot is retained only as a passive-arm excitation case.

</details>


### [237] [Freehand Sketching for End-User Programming of Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11078)
*Riwa Karam,Ian Kuo,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究以手绘草图作为机器人集群几何编队的终端用户编程接口，用户通过绘图表达空间意图，集群自动提取队形点、构建刚性编队图、分配机器人并执行带防镜像保证的分布式编队控制；20名参与者生成42个几何形状，SUS均分84.25，表明高可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群在应用中越来越需要非专家用户的可访问交互，而现有编程接口对无机器人或编程经验者门槛较高，因此探索手绘草图作为直观的编队指定方式。

Method: 提出草图到集群（sketch-to-swarm）框架：从用户手绘图提取目标队形点，构建刚性编队图，分配机器人到队形节点，并执行带防止非预期镜像队形保证的分布式编队控制；通过人类用户研究评估可用性。

Result: 20名参与者共生成42个几何形状，接口的System Usability Scale平均得分为84.25，按惯例属于高感知可用性。

Conclusion: 手绘草图可作为一种直观的人-集群协作交互抽象，无需机器人或编程专业知识即可指定集群几何编队。

Abstract: Robot swarms are increasingly used in applications where accessible interaction with non-expert users is desirable. This paper investigates freehand sketching as an end-user programming interface for specifying robot swarm geometries. Users communicate spatial intent through a drawing, while the swarm autonomously extracts target formation points, constructs a rigid formation graph, assigns robots to formation nodes, and executes distributed formation control with a guarantee against unintended reflected formations. The resulting sketch-to-swarm framework is evaluated through a human study examining the usability of freehand formation specification. Twenty participants generated $42$ geometric shapes, and the interface achieved a mean System Usability Scale score of $84.25$, which conventionally indicates high perceived usability. The results support freehand sketching as an intuitive interaction abstraction for human-swarm collaboration without requiring robotics or programming expertise.

</details>


### [238] [RIDE: Relocalization-Informed Depth Estimation with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11079)
*Jiarong Lian,Zhe Xiao,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Li,Ruizhi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIDE利用render-match-PnP重定位中得到的稀疏度量深度观测，结合预训练视频深度模型的几何先验，实现机器人RGB流的稠密度量深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: render-match-PnP重定位能建立查询图像像素与3D地图点的对应关系以恢复相机位姿，但其支持稠密深度估计的潜力常被忽视，本文希望挖掘这一几何信息。

Method: 给定度量的3DGS模型，将PnP-RANSAC内点对应关系导出的稀疏度量深度观测与预训练视频深度模型的几何先验相结合；针对观测不均与间歇问题，引入带时序记忆的全局和局部深度校正，支持度量尺度初始化后的短观测间隙下深度估计。

Result: 在公开RGB-D视频上训练，在机器人序列上无微调评估；实验显示相比仅尺度校准方法，深度精度和时序一致性均有提升。

Conclusion: 表明定位几何既能支持位姿恢复，也能支持稠密机器人感知。

Abstract: Render--match--PnP relocalization establishes correspondences between query image pixels and 3D map points for camera pose recovery, but their potential to support dense depth estimation is often overlooked. To exploit this geometric information, we present RIDE, which estimates dense metric depth from a robot's RGB stream. Given a metrically scaled 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, RIDE combines sparse metric depth observations derived from PnP-RANSAC inlier correspondences with the geometric prior of a pretrained video-depth model. To handle uneven and intermittent observations, it integrates global and local depth correction with temporal memory, supporting depth estimation through short observation gaps after metric scale initialization. Trained on public RGB-D videos, RIDE is evaluated on robot sequences without fine tuning. Experiments show improved depth accuracy and temporal consistency over scale-only calibration, demonstrating how localization geometry can support both pose recovery and dense robot perception.

</details>


### [239] [Harness Robotic OS: A Unified Embodied-Agent Runtime for Closed-Loop Quadruped Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11225)
*Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Haoxiang Jie,Gang Liu,Hongjie Yan,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的具身智能体运行时 HROS 及其住宅社区巡检实现 Argos，通过共享上下文、多模态交互、分层记忆和安全门控自演化，实现四足机器人巡检的闭环部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足巡检系统多以任务专用接口集成各类功能，导致上下文协调、知识复用与受控自适应困难，缺乏可追溯的运维闭环，需要统一的具身智能体运行时来整合异构感知、可复用自主能力、多模态场景理解、人机交互与企业级响应。

Method: HROS 划分为机器人运行时、具身自主技能、认知智能体运行时以及交互与运维平面四个层次；用共享上下文连接物理状态与智能体推理，流式 ASR/TTS 支持语音任务交互，构建工作记忆、情景记忆和语义记忆的分层记忆，并设计安全门控自演化环，将执行轨迹转化为版本化候选更新而非无约束在线修改。Argos 原型集成 Vbot 四足平台、Fast-LIO2 定位建图、Hobot-Stereo 深度感知、PCT-Planner 全局规划、EGO-Planner 局部运动生成及 OpenClaw 编排的 Qwen3-VL 巡检分析。

Result: 在住宅物业环境实验中，实现 100% 航点可达率，室外定位误差低于 10 cm，局部避障响应延迟低于 200 ms，代表性隐患检测率达 85--95%，报警投递与结构化报告生成成功率 99%。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了所部署的导航与巡检闭环，HROS 为具备记忆增强、语音感知和持续可改进能力的具身巡检智能体提供了可扩展的软件基础。

Abstract: Autonomous property inspection requires more than robust robot navigation: a deployable system must connect heterogeneous sensing, reusable autonomy capabilities, multimodal scene understanding, human interaction, and enterprise response within a traceable operational loop. Existing quadruped inspection systems commonly integrate these functions through task-specific interfaces, making contextual coordination, knowledge reuse, and controlled adaptation difficult. This paper presents \textit{Harness Robotic OS} (HROS), a unified embodied-agent runtime, and Argos, its realization for residential-community inspection. HROS organizes the system into robot runtime, embodied autonomy skills, cognitive agent runtime, and interaction and operations planes. A shared context connects physical state with agent reasoning; streaming ASR/TTS supports voice-based mission interaction; hierarchical working, episodic, and semantic memory preserves operational knowledge; and a safety-gated self-evolution loop converts execution traces into versioned candidate updates without permitting unconstrained online modification. The Argos prototype integrates a Vbot quadruped, Fast-LIO2 localization and mapping, Hobot-Stereo depth perception, PCT-Planner global planning, EGO-Planner local motion generation, and OpenClaw-orchestrated Qwen3-VL inspection analysis. Experiments in a residential property environment achieved 100\% waypoint reachability, outdoor localization error below 10~cm, local obstacle-response latency below 200~ms, representative hazard-detection rates of 85--95\%, and 99\% success in alarm delivery and structured-report generation. These results validate the deployed navigation and inspection closed loop, while HROS provides an extensible software foundation for memory-augmented, voice-aware, and continuously improvable embodied inspection agents.

</details>


### [240] [Beyond Noise Steering: Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning for Generative Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11270)
*Pengfei Zhang,Teng Sun,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DLSRL双潜空间强化学习框架，在冻结的生成器内同时进行初始噪声引导与表示级控制，加速在线机器人策略自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法仅对生成式机器人策略的噪声空间进行引导，无法在生成过程中调节中间动作表示，导致性能下降与效率低下。

Method: actor网络预测两个潜变量：一个初始噪声潜变量用于引导行为生成，一个动作表示潜变量用于中间特征调制；表示潜变量映射为适配器特征，通过残差连接注入中间动作token的隐藏状态，实现对动作表示的直接调整而无需更新基础策略。

Result: 在多种生成式策略架构与机器人操作任务上的实验表明，DLSRL有效加速了在线机器人策略自适应，并取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 双重控制设计可在不更新基础策略的前提下调节动作表示，为生成式机器人策略的在线强化学习微调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies learn expressive action priors from demonstrations. However, existing reinforcement learning methods only steer the noisy space but fail to modulate intermediate action representations during the generation process, resulting in performance degradation and inefficiency. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Dual-Latent Space Reinforcement Learning (DLSRL) framework, which complements initial-noise steering with representation-level control inside the frozen generator. Specifically, our actor network predicts two distinct latent variables: an initial-noise latent variable that steers behavior generation, and an action-representation latent variable for intermediate feature modulation. Moreover, this representation latent variable is mapped to adapter features and ingeniously injected into the hidden states of intermediate action tokens via residual connections. Our dual-control design enables direct adjustment of action representations without updating the base policy. Experiments across generative policy architectures and robotic manipulation tasks show that DLSRL effectively accelerates online robot policy adaptation and achieves competitive performance. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}{https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/DLSRL}.

</details>


### [241] [2AM: Grounding Agent-Side Memory as Guidance for Steerable Action Models in Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11308)
*Yutong Hu,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Renaud Detry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2AM提出让多模态Agent独自承担任务记忆、由单一RGB-based情景无状态Action Model执行动作的分离式设计，在LIBERO-Mem上达到76.3%平均完成率，较最强基线14.8%提升61.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要记忆，但记忆不必内嵌于动作策略中。现有Agentic系统常将VLA与规划器、几何工具结合，甚至引入深度或标定几何，导致归因混淆：性能提升可能来自更丰富的观测或替代运动工具，失败则可能源于策略或语言接口不明确。作者希望隔离这一问题，采用"工具广度更窄、接口带宽更大"的受约束设计来厘清任务记忆应归属于谁。

Method: 2AM采用双组件架构：多模态Agent作为任务记忆的唯一持有者，单一基于RGB、情景无状态的Action Model作为任务相关运动的唯一执行者。Agent将交互历史编译为子任务语言，并附加可选的2D抓取、放置与移动提示，在不同时间尺度上绑定其物理意图。为让VLA学会这种可操控性，作者在演示数据中增补结构化提示标签，并在条件dropout、空间噪声与时间抖动的训练设置下学习，以容忍Agent输出的不完美。

Result: 在LIBERO-Mem上，不使用深度、在线几何或基于规划器的物体运动，2AM取得76.3%的平均完成率，相比最强已报告基线14.8%提升61.5个百分点；同时达到63.0%的宽松成功率与11.8%的严格成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明任务记忆可以保留在Agent侧，无需内嵌到动作策略中。同时说明Action Model的能力不仅取决于策略学到了什么，还取决于Agent能够以多高的精度对其施加引导。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires memory, but not necessarily inside the action policy. To address such tasks, current agentic systems often combine VLAs with planners and geometric tools, sometimes using additional depth or calibrated geometry. These systems confound attribution: gains may come from richer observations or alternative motor tools, while failures may stem from either the policy or an under-specified language interface. We isolate this question through a deliberately constrained design: less tool breadth, but greater interface bandwidth. 2AM makes a multimodal Agent the sole holder of task memory and a single RGB-based, episodically stateless Action Model the sole executor of task-relevant motion. The Agent compiles interaction history into subtask language and optional 2D grasp, place, and move hints that bind its physical intention at different time scales. To teach this steerability to the VLA, we augment demonstrations with structured hint labels and train under condition dropout, spatial noise, and temporal jitter to tolerate imperfect Agent outputs. On LIBERO-Mem, without depth, online geometry, or planner-based object motion, 2AM reaches 76.3% average completion, a 61.5-point improvement over the strongest reported baseline of 14.8%, together with 63.0% relaxed and 11.8% strict success. These results show that task memory can remain Agent-side. They further show that Action Model capability depends not only on what the policy has learned, but on how precisely the Agent can steer it.

</details>


### [242] [Modular Kinematic Reduction of Closed-Chain Mechanisms Using Path Assembly and Defect Homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11338)
*Mohammad Dastranj,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACDM框架，通过路径装配闭包微分映射实现闭链模块化建模与运动学降维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭链结构通过非线性闭包约束耦合主被动坐标，给模块化建模与运动学降维带来困难，需要一种能统一处理闭包求解与降维的方法。

Method: 每个闭包单元比较两条具有公共端点的有序变换路径，利用SE(3)对数表示失配并组装局部变换导数Jacobian；由最小成对闭包单元构建多路径模块，用秩揭示分析选取局部独立标量约束；采用缺陷同伦沿可行正则连续路径恢复闭包一致的被动坐标；在正则构型处用隐式微分得到局部主动-被动微分映射，用于预测-校正连续过程。

Result: 在含两路径与三路径闭链模块的七自由度重型机械臂上评估，与Simscape Multibody对比轨迹均方根误差低于8.5×10^-10 rad，预测-校正连续比逐样本缺陷同伦快约45.8倍。

Conclusion: PACDM为模块化闭链求解与运动学降维提供统一、高效且高精度的框架。

Abstract: Closed kinematic chains complicate modular modeling by coupling active and passive coordinates through nonlinear closure constraints. This paper presents a Path-Assembled Closure Differential Mapping (PACDM) framework for modular closure resolution and kinematic reduction. Each closure element compares two ordered transformation paths with common endpoints, with their mismatch expressed through the logarithm on SE(3) and the corresponding Jacobian assembled from local transformation derivatives. Multi-path modules are constructed from a minimal set of pairwise closure elements, while rank-revealing analysis selects locally independent scalar constraints. A defect homotopy recovers closure-consistent passive coordinates from approximate estimates along a feasible and regular continuation path. At regular configurations, implicit differentiation yields the local active-to-passive differential mapping, which is subsequently used in a predictor-corrector continuation procedure for prescribed motion. The framework is evaluated on a seven-degree-of-freedom heavy-duty manipulator containing two-path and three-path closed-chain modules. Comparison with Simscape Multibody yields trajectory root-mean-square errors below 8.5 x 10^-10 rad, while predictor-corrector continuation is approximately 45.8 times faster than applying defect homotopy at every trajectory sample.

</details>


### [243] [Morphology-Aware Human Motion Retargeting for Wheeled-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11357)
*Chenbo Xia,Chao Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套从人类动作数据到轮式人形机器人Galaxea R1 Pro可执行行为（移动-操作耦合）的可复现重定向与学习流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类到人形的重定向研究多集中于足式平台，轮式人形系统在利用通用人类动作实现移动与操作耦合方面支持较少；同时R1 Pro无腿部关节，需将人类下肢运动合理分配到平面基座与躯干上，且不破坏与操作相关的臂部几何。

Method: 基于GMR可配置通用动作重定向与BeyondMimic的物理仿真R1 Pro学习框架，将多数据集SMPLX动作转为可执行行为。流程包括：规范化体型预处理、平面基座归一化、形态感知差分逆运动学、以肩为根的分层手臂重定向、针对弯腰和下蹲的连续躯干替代；再由参考扭转驱动规划层将平面基座运动解码为满足滞回、运动学连续性、加速度与执行器速率限制的三轮转向与滚动指令；最后在Isaac Lab中训练21维BaseDecode策略，采用方向性关节限位缩放、聚焦上半身跟踪与分阶段轮-地接触奖励。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明系统实现了从人类动作数据到可物理跟踪的轮式人形移动-操作完整桥接，而非仅可视化重定向器；定量策略比较安排在后续修订中。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了面向轮式人形R1 Pro的通用人类动作重定向与策略学习流水线，强调可复现性与物理可执行性，定量评估尚待补充。

Abstract: Human-to-humanoid retargeting has largely been studied on legged platforms, while comparatively few wheeled-humanoid systems support coupled locomotion and manipulation from general human motion. Building on GMR's configurable general-motion retargeting and BeyondMimic's physically simulated R1 Pro learning framework, we present a reproducible pipeline that converts multi-dataset SMPLX motion into executable loco-manipulation behavior for the Galaxea R1 Pro wheeled humanoid. The robot has a planar three-wheel base, a serial torso, and two arms but no leg joints, so human lower-body motion must be redistributed across base motion and torso posture without sacrificing manipulation-relevant arm geometry. Our pipeline combines canonical body-shape preprocessing, planar-base normalization, morphology-aware differential inverse kinematics, shoulder-rooted hierarchical arm retargeting, and continuous torso substitution for bending and squatting. A reference-twist-driven planning layer then decodes planar base motion into continuous three-wheel steering and rolling commands subject to hysteresis, kinematic continuity, acceleration, and actuator-rate limits. Finally, a 21-dimensional BaseDecode policy is trained in Isaac Lab with directional joint-limit scaling, focused upper-body tracking, and a staged wheel-contact reward. The resulting system provides a complete bridge from human motion data to physically trackable wheeled-humanoid loco-manipulation rather than a visualization-only retargeter; quantitative policy comparisons remain scheduled for a later revision.

</details>


### [244] [GeoTrussRover: Morphological Computation with Contact-Semantic Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11361)
*Muyuan Ma,Yi Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuanyan Zheng,Ruiqi Hu,Boxuan Ke,Zhenyu Chen,Yicong Lin,Xin Hao Yang,Daliang Xiao,Zhinan Hou,Wanhao Niu,Yuan Sun,Yan Yang,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoTrussRover将电驱动变几何桁架(VGT)、轮式底座与接触语义形态规划控制相结合，用四个接触语义原语描述21个构件的协调，并通过物理约束投影实现跨台阶高度的运动复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构机器人能在固定机体无法越障时改变接触几何，而变几何桁架可将形状变化分布到承载结构中，但将其与移动底盘耦合会带来高维协调难题。因此需要一种低维表示来存储并复用这种任务协调。

Method: 求解一次源越障过程，提取描述21个构件协调的四个接触语义原语；用物理约束投影将其适配到未见过的台阶高度（保持相同接触拓扑）；若所有相位可行则不重算整个运动，仅当某相位违反新物理约束时重算该相位；再用全空间QP跟踪适配后的运动并修正构件与车轮误差。

Result: 从0.10m迁移到0.075m时，目标函数评估次数比完全重算减少63.7%；接触相位可行性分析覆盖0.10至0.46m台阶高度（即1.08至4.97倍车轮半径），上界接近理论可行边界；电驱动样机可跨越2.11倍车轮半径。

Conclusion: 所得低维表示将任务协调存储在超冗余承载形态中，并可在运动过程中复用，验证了接触语义原语方法在VGT-移动底盘系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Reconfigurable robots can change their contact geometry when a fixed body cannot negotiate an obstacle. A variable-geometry truss (VGT) distributes this shape change through a load-bearing structure, but coupling it to a mobile base creates a high-dimensional coordination problem. GeoTrussRover combines an electrically actuated VGT, a wheeled base, and contact-semantic morphology planning and control. We solve one source traversal and extract four contact-semantic primitives that describe coordination among 21 members. Physics-constrained projection adapts them to unseen step heights with the same contact topology. When every phase remains feasible, adaptation does not recompute the complete motion. If one phase violates the new physical constraints, only that phase is recomputed. A full-space QP then tracks the adapted motion and corrects member and wheel errors. For transfer from 0.10m to 0.075m, the method reduces objective-function evaluations by 63.7% relative to full recomputation. Contact-phase feasibility analysis covers step heights from 0.10 to 0.46m, or 1.08 to 4.97 wheel radii, with the upper value near the theoretical feasible boundary. The electric prototype traverses 2.11 wheel radii. The resulting low-dimensional representation stores task coordination in a hyper-redundant, load-bearing morphology and reuses it during locomotion.

</details>


### [245] [SwarmNxt: Open-source Software-Hardware Platform for Fast and Agile Aerial Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11382)
*Charbel Toumieh,Niel Mistry,Benjamin Jarvis,Simon Jeger,Peize Liu,Shaojie Shen,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmNxt 是一个基于开源 OmniNxt 硬件的开源软件平台，为空中机器人集群提供端到端自主导航研究基础设施，并通过六机与四机实机实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人集群在安全、安保与搜救等时间敏感任务中潜力巨大，但实现鲁棒的自主集群导航仍是难题。现有商用无人机多为闭源，且缺乏敏捷视觉集群飞行所需的机载算力；同时多机软件的开发、部署与维护工程成本高昂，限制了研究进展。

Method: 提出 SwarmNxt，构建于开源 OmniNxt 无人机硬件之上的开源软件平台。提供端到端工具链：含视频教程的详细硬件组装说明、用于并行软件部署与集群级更新的自动化工具，以及基于 ROS 2 的自主导航框架；将先进的控制、规划与深度估计集成到单一 ROS 2 多智能体系统中。

Result: 两个真实世界实验验证：六机集群执行去中心化规划并实现高速机间避碰；四机集群在充满障碍物的环境中利用机载深度估计进行集体飞行。两项实验均在室内进行，全局定位来自外部动捕系统，感知、规划与控制均机载运行。

Conclusion: SwarmNxt 为物理集群实验提供了开放的研究基础设施，降低了多机自主飞行的开发与部署门槛，推动了鲁棒自主集群导航研究的进展。

Abstract: Aerial robot swarms have the potential to transform time-critical safety, security, and search-and-rescue operations. By coordinating multiple robots, they can rapidly survey disaster sites, map collapsed or GPS-denied environments, and search cluttered areas faster than a single robot, reducing response times and minimizing risks to first responders. Realizing this potential, however, requires robust autonomous swarm navigation, which remains an active research challenge. Progress is further constrained by existing platforms, as commercial drones are often closed-source or lack the onboard computational resources needed for agile, vision-based collective flight. Moreover, developing, deploying, and maintaining software across multiple aerial robots requires significant engineering effort. To address these challenges, we present SwarmNxt, an open-source software platform built on the open-source OmniNxt drone hardware. SwarmNxt provides an end-to-end toolkit, including detailed hardware assembly instructions with a video tutorial, automation tools for parallel software deployment and swarm-wide updates, and a ROS 2-based framework for autonomous navigation. The platform integrates state-of-the-art control, planning, and depth estimation into a single ROS 2 multi-agent system, providing an open research infrastructure for physical swarm experimentation. We validate SwarmNxt through two real-world experiments: a six-drone swarm performing decentralized planning with high-speed inter-drone collision avoidance, and a four-drone swarm executing collective flight with onboard depth estimation in an obstacle-filled environment. Both experiments were run indoors with global position from external motion capture; perception, planning, and control run onboard.

</details>


### [246] [Safety-aware Skill Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11433)
*A K M Nadimul Haque,Sheila Sutjipto,Marc G. Carmichael,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dist-GPRL，一个距离感知与安全引导的强化学习框架，用于结构化机器人技能自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的技能自适应框架通常依赖受限假设（如固定观测或严格控制的探索调度）来维持稳定性。在杂乱动态环境中，无约束探索易导致不安全行为与不稳定学习，尤其当任务相关观测靠近障碍物或涉及运动物体时。

Method: 基于高斯过程（GP）技能参数化，顺序自适应稀疏轨迹路点的重叠局部窗口，而非每步修改完整技能；原始策略输出通过 GP 协方差结构关联，生成时间一致的轨迹更新，降低动作空间与信用分配难度。安全引导包含两部分：来自 Hausdorff 近似规划器（HAP）的安全子空间先验，偏向可行区域探索；动态更新的距离场间隙与梯度奖励提供局部障碍感知。此外引入轨迹运动学相似性正则化器，保持示教的速度与加速度特性。

Result: 在仿真中的两个动态物体操作任务上评估，并将学习策略迁移到真实机器人执行。结果相比基线具有更高的任务成功率、更低的碰撞频率和更稳定的学习，同时保留了示教技能的运动学特性。

Conclusion: Dist-GPRL 通过距离感知与安全引导，实现了杂乱动态环境下结构化机器人技能的安全、稳定自适应。

Abstract: Skill adaptation frameworks based on reinforcement learning often require restrictive assumptions to maintain stability, such as fixed observations or tightly controlled exploration schedules.
